Our results show that, across both domains, merely disclosing sustainability information in explanations does not change choices, whereas framing that information or invoking a descriptive social norm significantly increases sustainable selections and eases decision-making. Notably, perception and behavior diverge, as plain disclosure improves explanation evaluations without translating into more sustainable selection behavior. Our work demonstrates how LLMs can generate theory-grounded explanations at scale, pointing toward practical explanation-based interventions for social good. We conclude by discussing implications for adaptive explanation design with generative AI.

</details>


### [241] [Loss Invariance Determines What Concept Layers Encode: Volume Grounding in Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25748)
*Hyunkyung Han,Min Jung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 概念瓶颈模型通过中间变量进行预测，但仅靠概念预测准确性不足以验证模型有效性。本研究以左心室容积作为射血分数估计的概念层，发现仅使用射血分数目标训练时，容积预测的分布坍缩至0.1毫升，原因在于射血分数作为比值对容积尺度具有不变性。添加容积监督虽小幅增加射血分数误差（0.4），但显著降低了容积误差（从89.8降至25.8毫升）。结论：概念准确性可能掩盖无物理尺度的概念层，需根据训练目标的不变性结构验证可解释中间变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究概念瓶颈模型中仅凭概念预测准确性判断模型有效性的做法是否充分，特别是在临床应用中物理尺度至关重要的场景。

Method: 使用视频Transformer编码器在公开超声心动图数据集上训练，以左心室收缩末期和舒张末期容积为概念层，通过解析计算射血分数（无残差路径）。对比仅使用射血分数目标训练与额外添加容积监督（毫升单位）两种方案，在1276个保留样本上评估。

Result: 概念瓶颈未增加射血分数误差（MAE 6.89 vs 7.13）。无容积监督时，容积预测分布坍缩至0.1毫升（参考标准差35.7和45.7毫升），但相关性部分保留。添加容积监督后容积误差从89.8降至25.8毫升，射血分数误差仅增加0.4。

Conclusion: 概念准确性可能掩盖概念层缺乏物理尺度的问题；可解释中间变量的验证应考量训练目标的不变性结构，而非仅依赖预测精度。

Abstract: Objective: Concept bottleneck models route prediction through interpretable intermediate variables, and their validity is normally judged by how accurately those variables are predicted. We ask whether that judgement is sufficient, using left ventricular volumes as the concepts underlying ejection fraction estimation from echocardiographic video.
  Methods: A video transformer encoder was trained on a publicly available echocardiography dataset. End-systolic and end-diastolic volumes formed a concept layer from which ejection fraction was computed analytically, with no residual path to the output. We compared training under an ejection fraction objective alone against training with additional supervision of the volumes in millilitres, and evaluated both on 1276 held-out studies.
  Results: The concept bottleneck did not increase ejection fraction error relative to direct regression, at 6.89 against 7.13 mean absolute error. Without volume supervision, however, the spread of predicted volumes collapsed to 0.1 millilitres against reference spreads of 35.7 and 45.7 millilitres, while correlation was partly preserved. We show that this follows from an invariance property of the objective: ejection fraction is a ratio and is unchanged when both volumes are rescaled, so the loss determines the concept layer only up to scale. Supervision in absolute units reduced volume error from 89.8 to 25.8 millilitres at a cost of 0.4 in ejection fraction error.
  Conclusion: Concept accuracy alone can conceal a concept layer that carries no physical scale.
  Significance: Interpretable intermediate variables in clinical models should be validated against the invariance structure of the training objective, not only against prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [242] [Speculate While You Reason: Teaching Agents to Predict Their Next Tool Call via Joint Agent-Speculator RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25816)
*Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Gungor Polatkan,Siyu Zhu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自推测智能体，将智能体与推测器统一为同一模型，通过联合智能体-推测器强化学习方法，在保持任务成功率的同时显著提升下一工具调用预测准确率（Hit@1提升约17个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在等待工具调用结果时会耗费大量墙钟时间。现有工具调用推测方法通常使用独立的草稿模型或缓存轨迹，与部署的智能体自身行为存在对齐偏差（推测器-智能体差距），导致推测准确性受限。

Method: 1) 提出自推测智能体（Self-Speculating Agent），同一模型同时具备智能体模式（解决任务）和推测模式（从部分轨迹预测下一工具调用）。2) 在推测模式下复用前缀KV缓存，避免额外推理开销。3) 提出联合智能体-推测器强化学习方法，从智能体自身 rollout 中推导推测目标，交替更新智能体和推测器。

Result: 在智能体搜索QA和对话式工具使用任务上，Qwen3-4B的下一工具调用Hit@1从44.1提升至61.2，Qwen3.5-4B从48.9提升至66.3，同时智能体任务成功率保持不变。

Conclusion: 目标智能体本身是最强的下一调用推测器，将智能体与推测器统一为同一模型能够有效消除推测器-智能体差距，在保持任务性能的前提下大幅提升推测准确率。

Abstract: Large language model agents often spend substantial wall-clock time waiting for tool call results. Tool-call speculation can hide this latency by predicting and pre-executing an agent's next tool call if the prediction matches the agent's eventual tool call, but existing speculators are typically separate draft models or cached traces that are poorly aligned with the deployed agent's own behavior. We identify this speculator-agent gap and show that the target agent itself is a strong next-call speculator. This points to a simpler design: unifying the agent and speculator within the same model. In this paper, we introduce the self-speculating agent, a single model that both solves tasks in agent mode and predicts its next tool call from partial trajectories in speculator mode, fully reusing prefix KV cache. To enable this dual-mode agent without degrading performance, we propose a joint agent-speculator reinforcement learning method, which derives speculation targets from the agent's own rollouts and alternates agent and speculator updates. Across agentic search QA and conversational tool-use agentic tasks, our method improves average next tool-call Hit@1 from 44.1 to 61.2 for Qwen3-4B and from 48.9 to 66.3 for Qwen3.5-4B, while preserving agent task success.

</details>


### [243] [HiSkill: Empowering LLM Agents with Hierarchical Skill Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25853)
*Yu Hao,Jinxuan Cai,Qi Zhang,Yawen Li,Zhiqiang Zhang,Chuan Shi,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiSkill提出了一种层次化技能图框架，将交互轨迹组织成包含技能节点、AtomicOp节点和类型化边的有向图，桥接高层技能与可执行动作，在三个交互环境中超越现有基线并降低推理token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹到技能的方法通常产生扁平的、独立存储和检索的高层文本技能集合，未能充分利用技能间关系，且高层技能与可执行动作之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 构建层次化技能有向图，包含技能节点和AtomicOp节点以及分解、时序转移、兼容性、支持和恢复等类型化边；推理时检索任务相关子图，利用符号任务状态、当前技能和子图引导LLM智能体切换技能、选择AtomicOp并迭代地执行动作。

Result: 在三个交互环境上的实验表明，HiSkill优于现有最先进基线，同时降低了推理阶段的token消耗。

Conclusion: 层次化技能图能够有效桥接高层技能与可执行动作的落地，提升长程交互任务中LLM智能体的表现和效率。

Abstract: Skills have become an important abstraction for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experience in long-horizon interactive tasks. However, existing trajectory-to-skill methods often produce flat collections of high-level textual skills that are stored and retrieved independently, leaving skill relations underutilized and maintaining a gap between high-level skills and executable actions. In this paper, we propose HiSkill, a hierarchical skill graph framework that organizes interaction trajectories into a directed graph with skill nodes, AtomicOp nodes, and typed edges. Specifically, the graph connects reusable high-level skills with executable action templates, while also capturing decomposition, temporal transition, compatibility, support, and recovery relations among them. At inference time, HiSkill retrieves a compact task-relevant subgraph and performs subgraph-guided task execution, where a symbolic task state, an active skill, and the retrieved subgraph guide the LLM agent to switch skills, select AtomicOps, and ground executable actions iteratively. Experiments on three interactive environments show that HiSkill outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference token consumption, demonstrating the effectiveness of bridging high-level skills and executable action grounding through a hierarchical skill graph. Our data and code is available at https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/HiSkill.

</details>


### [244] [Runtime Uncertainty Monitoring for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems Using Bayesian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25877)
*Bart Custers,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的多智能体系统框架，通过令牌级对数概率和贝叶斯网络实现不确定性传播与校准，用于精算风险建模中的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算工作流是高风险决策场景，LLM的概率性和智能体间的依赖关系会引入不可靠输出，可能导致错误风险评估、不公平定价和监管不合规，因此需要有效的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 构建多智能体框架，包含数据准备、建模、审查和解释等专用智能体，由中央枢纽协调；利用令牌级对数概率经长度归一化后转换为校准的任务级置信度估计，再融入贝叶斯网络进行不确定性传播。

Result: 该框架能够复现基线精算性能，同时提供对工作流稳定性和运行时不确定性传播的额外洞察。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM框架结合贝叶斯网络和对数概率校准方法，可有效支持精算风险建模中的不确定性量化，兼顾性能复现与可解释性。

Abstract: This paper investigates how multi-agent systems (MAS)-based on large language models (LLMs) can support actuarial risk modelling, with a particular focus on uncertainty quantification. Actuarial workflows represent a high-stakes decision-support setting where unreliable outputs may lead to incorrect risk assessment, unfair pricing, and regulatory non-compliance. To address uncertainty introduced by the probabilistic nature of LLMs and dependencies between agents, a multi-agent framework is proposed in which specialised agents perform data preparation, modelling, review, and explanation tasks under a central hub. The main contribution is a novel approach to uncertainty propagation using token-level log-probabilities and a Bayesian Network. Importantly, log probabilities are not treated as direct probabilities of correctness or task success. Instead, length-normalised log-probability summaries are transformed into calibrated task-level confidence estimates before incorporation into the Bayesian Network. Results show that the framework reproduces baseline actuarial performance while providing additional insight into workflow stability and runtime uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [245] [Distributing Security Controls Through Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25890)
*William Robert Gore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SHarD（Secure Harness Distribution），一个基于Pi agent harness构建的可分发安全护栏，通过嵌入操作系统沙箱、技能扫描和工具限制三类安全控制，在23项OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications测试中达到100%调整得分，与最佳安全配置的商业代理效果相当，且可通过单条安装命令分发到分布式用户群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理正以前所未有的速度被采用，但安全与风险问题仍是跨组织扩展代理AI的主要障碍。现有安全控制未系统化地分发给工程团队，供应商原生解决方案引入生态系统依赖，不一定适应每种部署场景。因此需要探索能否在商业AI编码代理上实现现成的安全控制，并通过自定义代理护栏扩展到分布式用户。

Method: 采用分阶段测试方法，在四种代理配置（两种有/无控制的商业代理、基线护栏、安全加固护栏）上进行测试，使用源自OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications的23项测试套件。SHarD基于Pi agent harness构建，嵌入OS沙箱、技能扫描、工具限制三类安全控制，通过单条安装命令分发。

Result: SHarD在调整后得分为100%，与最佳安全配置的商业代理持平，所有测试类别无回归。同时发现模型非确定性会产生不一致的安全结果，自主代理行为可能跨越系统边界，而OS沙箱可直接缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 现成的安全控制可以通过自定义代理护栏嵌入商业AI编码代理并扩展到分布式用户，SHarD证明了这一方法的有效性。提出了控制护栏适应性框架的初步特征，并识别了第三个待研究问题供未来探索。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at historic speed, yet security and risk concerns remain the primary barrier to scaling agentic AI across organizations. Existing security controls for coding agents are not systematically distributed to engineering teams, and vendor-native solutions introduce ecosystem dependencies that may not suit every deployment context. This paper investigates whether off-the-shelf security controls can be implemented on commercial AI coding agents and scaled to a distributed user base via a custom agent harness. A phased testing methodology was applied across four agent configurations --- two commercial agents with and without controls, a baseline harness, and a security-hardened harness --- using a 23-test suite derived from the OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. SHarD (Secure Harness Distribution), a distributable harness built on the Pi agent harness, demonstrated that three categories of security controls --- OS sandboxing, skill scanning, and tool restriction --- can be embedded and distributed via a single install command while retaining equivalent efficacy to direct installation on commercial agents. SHarD achieved an adjusted score of 100\%, matching the best securely configured commercial agent, with no regression across any test category. Notable observations include evidence that model non-determinism produces inconsistent security outcomes and that autonomous agent behavior can cross system boundaries in ways that OS sandboxing directly mitigates. Initial characteristics toward a control harness fitness framework are proposed, and a third research question is identified for future investigation.

</details>


### [246] [Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891)
*Stefan Krsteski,Charlotte Meyer,Guillaume Allegre,Tony O'Halloran,Alexandre Sallinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Messier是一个统一的大规模语料库，包含957,253条记录，涵盖30个基准、714个智能体、11,891个任务和74,205个验证器，用于标准化评估AI智能体在交互环境中的表现，并揭示了不同基准类型间的进展差异以及多验证器聚合规则对排名的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体评估存在任务、脚手架、验证器和评分规则碎片化的问题，导致实证结果难以比较；已有工作局限于狭窄场景、规模有限或需要昂贵的重跑成本。

Method: 构建Messier语料库，整合公开基准分数并补充六个专业和科学领域的五智能体运行结果；每条记录按模型、脚手架、环境、任务、验证器和聚合规则标准化，并附有SOC/NAICS职业和行业分类；通过反事实重新评分分析聚合规则影响，并推导能力量表。

Result: 前沿进展在不同基准类型间不均衡："函数调用"趋于饱和，"编程"进步最快，"企业工作流"最具挑战性；严格的全通过聚合规则会掩盖进展并人为改变智能体排名；能力量表与Epoch评估能力指数排名Spearman ρ=0.81。

Conclusion: Messier为智能体能力缩放、基准审计和评估失败细粒度分析提供了可复用的基础设施。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents in interactive environments is hindered by fragmented tasks, scaffolds, verifiers, and scoring rules. Existing efforts focus on narrow settings, remain limited in scale, or require costly reruns, leaving much of the empirical record incomparable. We introduce Messier, a unified corpus of 957,253 records that span 30 benchmarks, 714 agents, 11,891 tasks, and 74,205 verifiers. Messier consolidates public benchmark scores and supplements them with five-agent runs across six underrepresented professional and scientific domains, including a recent legal benchmark. Each record is standardized by model, scaffold, environment, task, verifier, and aggregation rule, with SOC/NAICS classifications for occupational and industry analysis. Using this corpus, we show frontier progress is uneven across benchmark types, with "function calling" saturated, "programming" improving the fastest, and "enterprise workflows" remaining the most challenging. Furthermore, counterfactual rescoring shows that strict all-pass aggregation in multi-verifier tasks can obscure progress and artificially alter agent rankings. From these standardized records, we derive capability scales that align with Epoch's Evaluation Capability Index rankings at Spearman \r{ho} = 0.81 and can be specialized by domain, occupation, action space, or verifier type. Messier provides a foundational, reusable infrastructure for agent capability scaling, benchmark auditing, and fine-grained analysis of evaluation failures.

</details>


### [247] [Interactive Reward Agent: GUI Task Evaluation via Environment-State Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25904)
*Chenrui Shi,Yuwei Wu,Yang Liu,Ruining Feng,Zirui Shang,Zhi Gao,Lifeng Fan,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出交互式奖励智能体（IRA），基于“提出-验证”框架，通过调用系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具从执行后环境中获取并验证证据，实现可靠的GUI任务评估。IRA在GUI-RewardBench上达到86.9%的准确率，并将其作为奖励信号用于强化学习训练GUI智能体，在OSWorld上取得34.0%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化GUI任务评估面临的核心挑战在于，判断任务是否完成往往需要访问执行后环境的状态信息（如系统配置、文件数据、应用设置等），而不仅仅是执行轨迹的截图。现有评估方法难以可靠获取这些深层环境证据。

Method: 提出交互式奖励智能体（IRA），采用“提出-验证”框架：首先根据任务指令提出任务完成条件，然后通过交互式地调用系统工具、应用工具和GUI工具从环境中获取并验证证据，综合可见界面与环境状态信息进行判断。

Result: IRA在包含321条GUI任务轨迹（覆盖10类Ubuntu桌面应用）的GUI-RewardBench上达到86.9%的准确率，显著优于现有评估基线。将IRA用于强化学习训练GUI智能体，在OSWorld上取得34.0%的成功率。

Conclusion: IRA能够为GUI智能体训练提供有效的奖励信号，证明了结合环境状态交互验证的评估方法在GUI任务评估中的有效性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface task evaluation aims to determine whether a GUI agent has successfully completed a user instruction. Automated GUI task evaluation has received increasing attention because the evaluation results can serve as reward signals for both test-time scaling and post-training. However, reliable GUI task evaluation remains challenging because the judgments often require access to environment states, such as system configurations, file data, and application settings, beyond the screenshots of execution trajectories. In this paper, we propose an interactive reward agent (IRA) based on a propose-then-verify framework to acquire and verify evidence from the post-execution environment. Given a task instruction and a GUI environment after the GUI agent execution, IRA first proposes the task completion conditions and then verifies them by invoking system tools, application tools, and GUI tools. This design combines evidence from both visible interfaces and the environment state in an interactive process. We further introduce GUI-RewardBench, a benchmark of 321 GUI task trajectories spanning 10 Ubuntu desktop application categories. Experiments show that IRA achieves 86.9% accuracy on GUI-RewardBench, outperforming existing evaluator baselines. We further apply IRA to reinforcement learning of GUI agents, achieving a 34.0% OSWorld success rate, which demonstrates that IRA can provide effective reward signals for training GUI agents.

</details>


### [248] [Toward Standardized Cross-Vendor Agent Tool Trust Management in Autonomous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25914)
*Ravi Kant Sharma,Ashutosh Uttam,Ajay Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自治网络L4-L5中跨厂商工具调用缺乏信任可见性的问题，提出AgentToolMO——一个基于3GPP NRM的信息模型，包含形式化信任状态机、阻尼级联传播、跨厂商通知机制和依赖图追溯评估，仿真证明可将影响半径从小时级压缩至近实时收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自治网络Level 4-5阶段，AI Agent需跨厂商调用工具且无需人工干预，但现有管理标准缺乏跨厂商信任可见性的标准化机制。当Vendor B的工具被攻陷时，Vendor A的Agent仍持续调用该工具，导致信任降级无法被感知，引发级联服务影响。

Method: 提出AgentToolMO信息模型，包括：(1) 形式化定义的信任状态机，具备可证明的渐进式强制策略；(2) 阻尼级联传播，保证有界收敛；(3) 通过现有MnS接口实现跨厂商信任通知；(4) 基于NRM依赖图遍历的追溯影响评估。

Result: 跨多厂商拓扑的仿真评估表明：标准化跨厂商通知将爆炸半径从小时级未检测传播压缩至近实时遏制（受限于MnS通知传递延迟）；级联传播在有界迭代内保证收敛；通知规模在厂商域间呈次线性扩展。

Conclusion: 该框架在现有3GPP管理基础设施内运行，复用现有协议，为可信的多厂商自治网络管理提供了标准化路径。

Abstract: Autonomous Network Levels 4-5 require AI agents to invoke tools across vendor boundaries without human oversight, yet existing management standards lack a standardized mechanism for cross-vendor trust visibility. When a tool from Vendor B is compromised, agents from Vendor A continue invoking it -- unaware of the trust degradation -- causing cascading service impact. We present AgentToolMO, a proposed 3GPP NRM information model for agent tool trust management. The model comprises: a formally defined trust state machine with provable graduated enforcement, damped cascade propagation with bounded convergence, cross-vendor trust notifications via existing Management Services (MnS) interfaces, and retroactive impact assessment through NRM dependency graph traversal. Simulation-based evaluation across multi-vendor topologies shows that standardized cross-vendor notifications reduce blast radius from hours-scale undetected propagation to near-real-time containment bounded by MnS notification delivery, with cascade convergence guaranteed in bounded iterations and sub-linear notification scaling across vendor domains. The framework operates within existing 3GPP management infrastructure, leverages existing protocols, and provides a standardization pathway for trustworthy multi-vendor autonomous network management.

</details>


### [249] [Penelope: Localized Latent Recurrence for Efficient Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25915)
*Yutong Chen,Shouqian Shi,Xinran Liu,Haochen Wang,Jiaying Wang,Tianxing Xu,Yuanxi Wang,Zirui Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penelope是一种面向预训练解码器-only Transformer的高效潜在推理框架，通过将循环计算局部化到选定的解码器区间，利用时间调制GRU动力学和渐进式CoT-to-潜在课程，在不重复执行完整解码器或生成长中间轨迹的情况下，在潜在空间中分配额外计算，实现了精度与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型处理复杂结构化推理任务时，增加参数规模导致训练和部署成本高昂，而链式思维(CoT)将推理计算与自回归输出长度绑定，效率低下。

Method: 将循环计算局部化到选定的解码器区间：下部分解码器一次计算构建问题条件边界记忆，通过时间调制GRU动力学和循环读出状态迭代精化，再通过渐进式CoT-to-潜在课程将可见推理迁移到内部循环路径。

Result: 在开放源码的结构化推理基准上，在验证选择的潜在预算下，Penelope达到了与已有潜在推理模型相当的精度，同时降低了测量推理延迟。

Conclusion: 潜在精化可以局部化到狭窄的解码器区间，减少重复的全解码器执行，无需生成可见推理轨迹，为解码器-only Transformer模型提供了实用的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Complex structured reasoning tasks often require additional computation, yet current language models obtain it mainly by increasing parameter scale or by serializing intermediate steps as chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens. The former raises training and deployment costs, while the latter ties reasoning computation to autoregressive output length. We introduce Penelope, an efficient latent-reasoning framework for pretrained decoder-only Transformers that localizes recurrent computation to a selected decoder interval. The lower decoder prefix is evaluated once to construct a problem-conditioned boundary memory, which is then iteratively refined through time-modulated GRU dynamics and recurrent readout states before answer generation. A progressive CoT-to-latent curriculum transfers visible reasoning into this internal recurrent path, allowing additional computation to be allocated in latent space without repeatedly executing the complete decoder or generating a long intermediate trace. Experiments on open-source structured-reasoning benchmarks show that, at validation-selected latent budgets, Penelope attains competitive accuracy relative to established latent-reasoning models while reducing measured inference latency. These results show that latent refinement can be localized to a narrow decoder interval, reducing repeated full-decoder execution without generating a long visible reasoning trace and providing a practical accuracy-efficiency tradeoff for decoder-only Transformer models.

</details>


### [250] [dtControl2+$\varepsilon$: Trading Optimality for Explainability in MDPs via Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25925)
*Tereza Kinská,Jan Křetínský,Tobias Meggendorfer,Sabine Rieder,Maximilian Weininger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对MDP中的决策树控制器可解释性问题，提出ε-近似方法（dtControl2扩展），通过允许ε≥0的精度损失构建更小的决策树，在保证ε-最优性的同时大幅简化控制器表示，实现可调简化程度，生成比现有技术小几个数量级的决策树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策树控制器（如dtControl2）在大规模系统或存在大量边界情况时仍过于复杂，难以被人类理解。直接减小决策树规模可能导致遗漏关键情况，产生错误控制器，因此需要在保证正确性的前提下简化表示。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架下扩展dtControl2工具，引入ε功能：允许预设非负精度阈值ε，在保证控制器ε-最优性的约束下，通过可控的精度损失来压缩决策树规模，提取控制器核心本质。

Result: 构建的决策树比现有最先进工具（dtControl2）小若干个数量级，在保持ε-最优性保证的同时实现了显著简化。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效在控制器可解释性与最优性之间取得平衡，通过可调的简化程度为用户提供更易于理解的决策树表示，适用于大规模或复杂系统的控制器解释。

Abstract: Over the past decade, decision trees have been used to represent controllers (a.k.a. policies) in an explainable way, with dtControl2 as a current state-of-the-art tool. However, for systems that are large or have many corner cases, even such representations tend to be too complex and not human-comprehensible. Unfortunately, reducing the size of the decision tree is not straightforward, as missing just a single crucial case might result in an incorrect controller. We tackle this issue in the setting of Markov decision processes, extending dtControl2 by "$\varepsilon$" functionality: Given an allowed imprecision $\varepsilon \geq 0$, we construct a smaller decision tree, distilling the essence of the controller, while still guaranteeing its $\varepsilon$-optimality. This enables us to provide tunably simpler explanations, omitting a controllable amount of detail. Our tool constructs decision trees that are orders of magnitude smaller than the state of the art.

</details>


### [251] [Large Language Model for Operations Research Formulation Selection in Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25956)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解器引导的大语言模型（LLM）框架，用于多仓库库存分配中的运筹学（OR）配方选择问题。该框架将候选OR专家库中的混合整数规划（MIP）配方视为不同分配优先级编码，通过平衡专家条件的监督微调（SFT）进行模式学习，再结合MIP求解器评估结果构建边际加权身份偏好优化（IPO）偏好及专家评分元数据，最终通过群体相对策略优化（GRPO）对采样响应进行奖励分配。在京东多仓库库存分配实例上的实验表明，GRPO显著提升了专家选择准确率（Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%），分配准确率比现有基线提升12.57个百分点，与事后最优oracle的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多仓库库存分配通常被建模为混合整数规划（MIP）问题，但没有任何单一配方能够始终匹配由需求集中度、库存不平衡、补货规模、服务约束和预测波动性所导致的异构实例级场景。因此，需要研究实例级运筹学（OR）配方选择问题，为每个分配实例从候选OR专家库中分配一个可执行的配方。

Method: 提出求解器引导的大语言模型（LLM）框架，包含两个阶段：首先构建均衡专家条件的监督微调（SFT）记录进行模式学习；然后利用MIP求解器对历史实例的评估结果，将分配质量差距转化为边际加权身份偏好优化（IPO）偏好，并生成每个实例的专家评分元数据，在群体相对策略优化（GRPO）中为采样响应分配奖励。

Result: 在京东多仓库库存分配实例上，GRPO相对于SFT+IPO选择器显著提升了专家选择准确率：Hit Ratio@1从21.45%提升至50.42%，Hit Ratio@2从70.47%提升至82.31%。选择器分配准确率比现有基线提升12.57个百分点，优于SFT+IPO选择器和最佳固定OR专家，与事后最优oracle的差距缩小至4.85个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提出的求解器引导LLM框架能够有效解决多仓库库存分配中的OR配方选择问题，GRPO策略显著优于SFT+IPO及固定配方基线，大幅提升了分配质量和专家选择准确性。

Abstract: Multi-warehouse inventory allocation is typically formulated as a mixed-integer programming (MIP) problem, yet no single formulation consistently matches heterogeneous instance-level regimes induced by demand concentration, inventory imbalance, replenishment scale, service constraints, and forecast volatility. We study this issue as instance-wise operations research (OR) formulation selection, where each allocation instance is assigned to a solver-executable formulation from a candidate OR expert library. We propose a solver-guided large language model (LLM) framework for OR formulation selection, in which each OR expert corresponds to a MIP formulation encoding a distinct allocation priority. To train the selector, the framework first constructs balanced expert-conditioned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) records for schema learning, and then uses MIP solver evaluation on historical instances to convert solver-evaluated allocation-quality gaps into margin-weighted identity preference optimization (IPO) preferences and per-instance expert-score metadata for reward lookup during group relative policy optimization (GRPO) to assign rewards to sampled responses. Experiments on multi-warehouse inventory allocation instances from JD$\mathord{.}$com, one of China's largest e-retailers, demonstrate that GRPO substantially improves expert-selection accuracy relative to the SFT+IPO selector and, more importantly, produces higher realized allocation quality than both the preference-trained selector and the best fixed formulation. With GRPO, Hit Ratio@1 and Hit Ratio@2 increase from 21.45% to 50.42% and from 70.47% to 82.31%. The resulting selector achieves an allocation accuracy gain of 12.57 percentage points over the incumbent baseline, outperforming both the SFT+IPO selector and the best fixed OR expert, and reduces the gap to the ex-post oracle to 4.85 percentage points.

</details>


### [252] [CHARM: A Multimodal Graph Foundation Model with Hierarchical Context Modeling for Zero-Shot Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26023)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Yuxiao Huang,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHARM是一种多模态图基础模型，通过分层上下文建模实现零样本迁移，将原始节点替换为分层图上下文以捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系，无需目标域微调即可在未见过的领域进行知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界图数据常包含文本、图像等多模态信息，但现有图基础模型（基于GNN或LLM）在多模态图上的零样本迁移能力不足，要么需要目标域适应，要么仅处理单模态图或单一领域任务。

Method: 提出CHARM模型，核心是用分层图上下文替代孤立原始节点，捕获多模态语义和跨模态关系；通过模态感知的图上下文编码器融合多模态信息与图结构，将表示转化为图token供大语言模型处理。

Result: 在零样本多模态图任务上实现了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 分层上下文建模能有效将领域特定的节点模式映射到共享的高层概念，减少对目标域监督或适应的依赖，为多模态图零样本迁移提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for transferring knowledge across graph domains and tasks. Real-world graphs associate nodes with text, images, and other modalities, making multimodal graphs essential for representing complex entities and relations. Moreover, collecting labels and adapting models for every new graph domain is costly and often infeasible, motivating zero-shot transfer. Unfortunately, zero-shot transfer on multimodal graphs remains underexplored. Existing GNN-based graph foundation models typically require downstream adaptation, whereas LLM-based graph methods mainly address unimodal graphs or tasks within a single domain. This setting presents two key challenges. First, models must generalize knowledge from individual modalities while capturing transferable cross-modal relations. Second, without target-domain fine-tuning, node representations remain entangled with domain-specific structures and modality-specific characteristics, obscuring shared concepts in unseen domains. To address these challenges, we propose CHARM, a multimodal graph foundation model with hierarchical context modeling for zero-shot transfer. CHARM replaces isolated raw nodes with hierarchical graph contexts that capture multimodal semantics and cross-modal relations. These contexts map domain-specific node patterns to shared high-level concepts, reducing reliance on target-domain supervision or adaptation. A modality-aware graph context encoder integrates multimodal information with graph structure and converts the resulting representations into graph tokens for a large language model . Experiments show consistent improvements on zero-shot multimodal graph tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Beam-Response Contrastive Learning for Transmitter-Side MIMO CSI Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24872)
*Sehyun Ryu,Yumin Kim,Minjae Lee,Hyun Jong Yang,John M. Cioffi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出BRCL，一种基于发射端Gram矩阵的自监督CSI预训练框架，通过波束响应对比学习实现无标签的MIMO表征学习，在波束选择、用户选择等任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSI自监督预训练方法使用重建目标或通用增广的对比对，未能显式反映与传输相关的信道相似性，导致表征可迁移性受限。

Method: BRCL将每个CSI样本映射到波束响应分布，利用Gram矩阵诱导的软相似度作为无标签关系目标进行对比预训练，并结合重建学习同时保证样本级CSI恢复和波束响应级一致性。

Result: 在多种MIMO信道数据集上，BRCL在波束选择、用户选择和未来波束选择任务中均优于自编码器和信道图表等预训练方法，显著提升标签效率。

Conclusion: BRCL通过显式建模传输相关的信道相似性，实现了无需任务标签或人工正样本对的可迁移CSI表征学习，为MIMO系统自监督预训练提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Self-supervised representation learning from unlabeled channel state information (CSI) can reduce labeling and adaptation overhead in learning-based multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. Existing CSI pretraining methods typically use reconstruction objectives or contrastive pairs from generic augmentations, which do not explicitly reflect transmission-relevant channel similarity. This paper proposes beam-response contrastive learning (BRCL), a self-supervised CSI pretraining framework based on the transmit-side Gram matrix. For a channel matrix $\mathbf{H}$, $\mathbf{R}=\mathbf{H}^{\mathrm{H}}\mathbf{H}$ determines the received power of any unit-norm transmit beam $\mathbf{w}$ as $|\mathbf{H}\mathbf{w}|_2^2=\mathbf{w}^{\mathrm{H}}\mathbf{R}\mathbf{w}$. BRCL maps each CSI sample to a beam-response profile and uses the induced soft similarity as a label-free relational target for contrastive pretraining. Combined with reconstruction learning, BRCL enforces both sample-level CSI recovery and beam-response-level consistency, yielding transferable CSI representations without task-specific labels or manual positive pairs. Experiments on diverse MIMO channel datasets show that BRCL improves label efficiency and outperforms autoencoder- and channel-charting-based pretraining across beam selection, user selection, and future beam selection tasks.

</details>


### [254] [Low-Latency Generative Semantic Communication via Channel-Realization Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24876)
*Fan Gao,Youzheng Wang,Zhijin Qin,Feifei Gao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-BFM方法，将语义通信接收端恢复建模为实现耦合桥流匹配问题，利用物理信道实现耦合最优传输计划替代独立耦合，相比扩散接收机降低10倍以上解码延迟，在CIFAR-10和FFHQ-64×64上AWGN和瑞利衰落信道下实现了更优的保真度-感知质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语义通信接收机虽然能提供高感知质量，但存在解码延迟过高的瓶颈。扩散接收机依赖随机迭代解码，而现有流匹配接收机采用独立端点耦合，忽略了物理源-信道链路，导致采样轨迹不必要地长且弯曲。

Method: 提出实现耦合桥流匹配（RC-BFM），解码器从信道诱导的语义状态而非各向同性噪声初始化。训练对通过实现耦合熵最优传输（RC-OT）计划链接，保留每次传输的物理信道实现同时保持对随机衰落的鲁棒性。揭示了独立耦合是条件流匹配接收机中条件训练-测试分布偏移的根本原因，并推导了端到端失真界，其离散化误差以O(K^{-2})衰减。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和FFHQ-64×64上AWGN和瑞利衰落信道下，RC-BFM实现了更优的保真度-感知质量权衡，解码延迟相比扩散接收机降低10倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过将物理信道实现信息引入流匹配框架，RC-BFM有效解决了生成式语义接收机的解码延迟瓶颈，为低延迟高保真语义通信提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Generative semantic communication receivers deliver high perceptual quality but suffer from prohibitive decoding latency. This bottleneck arises because diffusion receivers rely on stochastic iterative decoding, while existing flow matching receivers employ independent endpoint coupling that ignores the physical source--channel link, yielding unnecessarily long and curved sampling trajectories. In this paper, we reformulate receiver-side recovery as a realization-coupled bridge flow matching problem under explicit bandwidth and power constraints. Specifically, we propose Realization-Coupled Bridge Flow Matching (RC-BFM), where the decoder initializes from a channel-induced semantic state rather than isotropic noise. Crucially, training pairs are linked via a realization-coupled entropic optimal transport (RC-OT) plan that preserves the physical channel realization of each transmission while maintaining robustness to stochastic fading. Furthermore, we identify independent coupling as the fundamental source of a conditional train--test distribution shift in conditional flow matching-based receivers, and derive an end-to-end distortion bound whose discretization error decays as \(O(K^{-2})\). Experiments on CIFAR-10 and FFHQ-64\(\times\)64 over AWGN and Rayleigh fading channels demonstrate that RC-BFM achieves a superior fidelity--perception trade-off, reducing decoding latency by over 10\(\times\) compared to diffusion-based receivers.

</details>


### [255] [High-Performance Reinforcement-Learned BP Decoding of Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24891)
*Mohsen Moradi,Vahid Nourozi,Taejoon Kim,Remi A. Chou,David G. M. Mitchell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-S2LU解码器，通过在BP解码中引入二阶局部更新和强化学习顺序调度，显著提升了量子LDPC码的纠错性能，同时保持了BP的局部性和低复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的泛洪BP解码在量子LDPC码上因稳定子简并性和短环而性能停滞，需要一种能自适应调度更新顺序且保持低复杂度的解码方案来提升收敛性。

Method: 将强化学习离线学习的顺序调度（RL-S）扩展为二阶局部更新解码器（RL-S2LU），在保持BP消息传递局部性的前提下，对可变节点进行二阶局部更新。

Result: 数值结果显示RL-S2LU相比传统BP和BP-OSD-10基线取得了显著的纠错增益。

Conclusion: RL-S2LU在保持BP局部性和低复杂度的同时，通过二阶局部更新有效提升了量子LDPC码的解码性能。

Abstract: Belief-propagation (BP) decoding is attractive for quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes because it uses local message passing on sparse Tanner graphs. However, conventional flooding BP often stalls due to stabilizer degeneracy and short cycles. Reinforcement-learning-based sequential variable-node scheduling (RL-S), which learns the update order offline, has shown that adaptive scheduling can improve BP convergence. In this paper, we extend this idea with a second-order local update decoder, RL-S2LU. The proposed decoder preserves BP locality and low complexity, while numerical results show significant error-correction gains over conventional BP and the considered BP-OSD-10 baseline.

</details>


### [256] [Learned Blockwise Port Activation for Real Time Beamforming in Fluid Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25365)
*Yuanhui Wu,Zhentian Zhang,Hanjiang Hong,Hao Jiang,Zaichen Zhang,Kai-Kit Wong,Yin Xu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出学习式块状端口激活（L-BPA）方法，用于流体天线阵列（FAA）下行多用户波束赋形。L-BPA通过在每个孔径块中激活固定数量的端口，结合轻量级卷积网络评分和多尺度几何排斥机制，在实时性下实现旁瓣感知的端口选择。相比均匀稀疏激活，平均峰值旁瓣电平降低3.26 dB且和速率略高；相比贪心法和增益法，PSLL分别降低8.13 dB和10.10 dB，且无需迭代在线搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAA多用户下行传输需要激活一组可重构端口，但激活掩码同时影响有效信道和稀疏辐射孔径，需在总速率、旁瓣抑制、硬件约束和在线复杂度之间取得平衡。现有方法中，信道驱动选择会导致端口聚集和旁瓣升高，而旁瓣导向合成通常与信道无关，会牺牲总速率。

Method: L-BPA采用分块激活策略，每个孔径块激活固定端口数，支持分组开关硬件并限制端口聚集。使用轻量级卷积网络根据多用户信道特征、端口坐标和用户功率统计对端口评分。训练结合分块直通掩码和可微峰值旁瓣电平（PSLL）代理。推理时，学习评分与多尺度几何排斥结合，再在缩减的有效信道上进行正则化迫零预编码。

Result: 相比均匀稀疏激活，平均PSLL降低3.26 dB，且和速率略高；相比贪心选择和增益选择，PSLL分别降低8.13 dB和10.10 dB，且无需迭代在线搜索。

Conclusion: L-BPA能在实时条件下有效平衡旁瓣抑制与总速率，优于现有方法，是一种实用的FAA下行波束赋形方案。

Abstract: Fluid antenna arrays (FAAs), support multiuser downlink transmission by activating a subset of reconfigurable ports. The activation mask jointly determines the effective channel and the sparse radiating aperture, which requires a balance among sum rate, sidelobe suppression, hardware constraints, and online complexity. Channel driven selection can cluster active ports and increase sidelobes, whereas sidelobe oriented synthesis is typically channel independent and can sacrifice sum rate. This paper proposes learned blockwise port activation (L-BPA), for real time sidelobe aware FAA downlink beamforming. L-BPA activates a fixed number of ports in each aperture block, which supports grouped switching hardware and limits port clustering. A lightweight convolutional network scores ports using multiuser channel features, port coordinates, and user power statistics. Training combines blockwise straight through masks with a differentiable peak sidelobe level (PSLL), surrogate. During inference, learned scores are combined with multiscale geometric repulsion, followed by regularized zero forcing precoding over the reduced effective channel. L-BPA reduces the average PSLL by 3.26 dB relative to uniform sparse activation while achieving a slightly higher sum rate. It also reduces the PSLL by 8.13 dB and 10.10 dB relative to greedy and gain based selection, respectively, without iterative online search.

</details>


### [257] [Bayesian-Guided Cooperative RL Beamforming for Wireless Adversarial User Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25417)
*Parmida Geranmayeh,Onur Günlü*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对下一代无线网络中的联合波束赋形与安全感知决策问题，提出了一种基于强化学习的框架。在3GPP系统模型下，分别评估了无攻击者和有攻击者场景，以及不同网络可扩展性条件下的性能。实验表明，Q-learning和SARSA方法在信道容量、攻击检测准确率和性能稳定性方面均优于随机选择，其中Q-learning在检测准确率和计算效率之间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络不仅需要提供高速率和高效率，还需同时保障通信安全。传统方法难以在动态环境中实时适应信道变化和用户行为，因此需要引入机器学习方法，实现智能、实时的网络管理框架。

Method: 首先在3GPP系统模型下，基于无攻击者场景使用穷举搜索作为基准确定最优波束赋形配置。随后引入攻击者，评估不同网络可扩展性条件下的性能。采用Q-learning和SARSA两种强化学习方法进行波束方向的自适应选择，并与随机选择策略进行对比。

Result: 强化学习方法（Q-learning和SARSA）在总信道容量、攻击检测准确率和性能稳定性方面均一致优于随机选择。其中Q-learning在检测准确率与计算效率之间取得了最佳整体权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的强化学习框架为动态和对抗性无线环境中的联合波束赋形与安全感知决策提供了一种稳定、可扩展且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In next-generation wireless networks, communication systems are expected to go beyond simple data transmission and simultaneously provide high data rates, efficiency, and security. This requirement has motivated the extensive adoption of machine learning methods to develop intelligent and real-time network management frameworks, enabling the system to continuously monitor and react to channel variations and user behavior while maintaining efficient information delivery. In this context, the integration of machine learning with beamforming enables adaptive and data-driven beam direction selection, improving both the efficiency and security of wireless links. In this work, a 3GPP-based system model is first implemented under a no-attacker scenario, and an exhaustive search is employed as a reference to identify the best beamforming configurations. The proposed framework is then evaluated in the presence of an attacker and under different network scalability conditions. We demonstrate that the reinforcement learning-based approaches, namely Q-learning and SARSA (State-Action-Reward-State-Action), consistently outperform random selection in terms of total channel capacity, attacker detection accuracy, and performance stability. Among the evaluated reinforcement learning methods, Q-learning achieves the best overall trade-off between detection accuracy and computational efficiency. Our results indicate that the proposed framework provides a stable, scalable, and effective solution for joint beamforming and security-aware decision-making in dynamic and adversarial wireless environments.

</details>


### [258] [System-Aware Adaptive CSI Feedback via RL-Guided Autoencoder Switching in Multi-User MIMO System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25588)
*Maryam Ansarifard,Mohit K. Sharma,George Exarchakos,Kishor C. Joshi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的系统感知自适应CSI反馈框架，通过在多速率自编码器池中选择最优压缩比，在动态MIMO信道条件下实现了反馈开销、重建精度和频谱效率三者之间的动态优化平衡，相比固定方案将CSI反馈成本降低53.4%，下行和速率提升53.64%，NMSE降低22.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于深度学习的CSI压缩使用固定压缩比，无法有效适应非平稳信道条件，导致在动态MIMO环境中反馈开销与重建保真度之间的权衡不够灵活高效。

Method: 构建一个由多个预训练多速率自编码器组成的压缩模型库，并设计集中式强化学习智能体，在每个时间步根据观测到的信道条件和系统性能指标，为每个用户选择最合适的压缩比；采用系统感知的奖励函数，联合考虑SINR反映的频谱效率、反馈开销约束和模型适应计算成本。

Result: 在高维时延域CSI数据集上，相比固定压缩方案和自适应基线，该方法平均降低CSI反馈成本53.4%，提升平均下行和速率53.64%，降低NMSE 22.38%。

Conclusion: 所提出的RL引导框架能够在动态无线条件下实现更高效的速率-精度-反馈三者权衡，同时保持适度的计算和内存开销，适用于实际大规模MIMO系统。

Abstract: This paper proposes a system-aware adaptive channel state information (CSI) feedback framework for massive multiple-input multiple-output (mMIMO) systems, aiming to dynamically optimize the trade-off between reconstruction fidelity and signaling overhead. While deep learning-based autoencoders (AEs) have enabled significant CSI compression, conventional fixed-ratio schemes fail to adapt effectively to non-stationary channel conditions. To address this limitation, we develop a reinforcement learning (RL)-driven control framework that operates over a bank of pretrained multi-rate AEs, each corresponding to a distinct compression ratio (CR). At each time step, a centralized RL agent selects the most suitable CR for each user based on observed channel conditions and system performance indicators. Distinct from conventional mean squared error (MSE)-centric designs, we introduce a system-aware reward formulation that jointly accounts for spectral efficiency via signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR), feedback overhead constraints, and the computational cost of model adaptation. Simulation results on high-dimensional delay-domain CSI datasets demonstrate that the proposed RL-guided framework effectively balances the overhead-accuracy tradeoff and adapts to dynamic channel environments. The proposed method improves spectral efficiency and feedback efficiency compared with fixed compression schemes and adaptive baselines, while maintaining a modest computational and memory footprint. Averaged over different numbers of users and across all considered baselines, the proposed RL framework reduces the CSI feedback cost by more than 53.4%, improves the average downlink sum rate by 53.64%, and reduces the NMSE by 22.38%. These results demonstrate its ability to achieve a more efficient rate-accuracy-feedback tradeoff under dynamic wireless conditions.

</details>


### [259] [Universal Individual-Sequence Prediction with a Primitive-Recursive Superpredictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25712)
*Amir Leshem*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对零一损失下的个体二元序列预测问题，构建了一个可计算概率预测器，该预测器相对于所有原始递归预测器（包括有限状态、上下文和PPM规则）在每个个体序列上具有显式的次线性遗憾界。进一步证明PPM预测器是原始递归的，从而在可计算平稳遍历二元源的每个Martin-Löf随机实现上达到无限过去贝叶斯误差，并建立了与有限状态预测和固定原始递归预测器的严格分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可计算主算法无法在所有序列上与所有全可计算预测器竞争，因此需要寻找一个更广泛但可处理的预测器类别（原始递归预测器），并构建一个与之竞争的可计算概率预测器。

Method: 利用原始递归预测器的句法可枚举性，构造可计算概率预测器并推导显式次线性遗憾界；证明PPM预测器属于原始递归类，从而将结果泛化到该著名预测算法；通过分离定理刻画不同预测器类别的能力边界。

Result: 成功构造了相对于所有原始递归预测器具有次线性遗憾的可计算概率预测器；PPM预测器被证明是原始递归的；该预测器在可计算平稳遍历二元源的Martin-Löf随机实现上达到无限过去贝叶斯误差；建立了与有限状态预测和固定原始递归预测器的严格分离。

Conclusion: 本文为个体序列预测提供了一种普适性理论框架，证明了原始递归预测器类是一个自然且足够广泛的可处理类别，并展示了所构造预测器在多个最优性准则下的表现。

Abstract: We study sequential prediction of individual binary sequences under zero-one loss. No computable master can compete on every sequence with all total computable predictors. We therefore consider rational-valued primitive-recursive forecasters, a broad syntactically enumerable class containing finite-state, context-based, and Prediction by Partial Matching (PPM) rules. We construct a computable probabilistic predictor with an explicit sublinear regret bound relative to every primitive-recursive forecaster on every individual sequence.
  We further prove that the PPM predictor is primitive recursive. Consequently, our predictor attains the infinite-past Bayes error on every Martin-Löf random realization of every computable stationary ergodic binary source. This optimality extends to finitely many independent such sources interleaved according to an arbitrary primitive-recursive schedule. Finally, we establish strict separations from finite-state prediction and from every fixed primitive-recursive predictor.
  keywords: Universal prediction, individual sequences, prediction with expert advice, primitive recursive functions, Kolmogorov complexity, PPM.

</details>


### [260] [The Rate-Distortion-Deception Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25997)
*Semih Akkoc,Sahan Liyanaarachchi,Sennur Ulukus,Aylin Yener*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的“欺骗约束”（deception constraint）指标，研究在保证重构样本与原始观测保持一定保真度的同时，使重构样本看起来来自另一个目标分布，并探索了在这种新约束下的率失真基本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的率失真理论在重构时只考虑与原始分布的保真度（失真约束）和与原始分布的一致性（感知约束），但实际应用中可能需要重构样本看起来来自另一个不同分布（例如隐私保护或风格迁移场景）。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将“欺骗约束”定义为一种新的指标，要求重构样本在满足对原始观测的保真度约束的同时，其分布接近于一个指定的目标分布（而非原始分布）。在此基础上，推导了率失真与欺骗之间的基本权衡关系。

Result: 建立了在欺骗约束下的率失真函数，给出了率失真与欺骗之间的理论权衡边界。

Conclusion: 欺骗约束扩展了传统率失真理论，为需要重构样本符合不同分布的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The problem of finding the optimal compression rate for a given random variable has been traditionally studied under two main constraints: distortion and perception. The distortion constraint enforces the fidelity of our reconstruction with respect to the observed realization of the random variable, while the perception constraint ensures that the reconstruction is close to a sample from the distribution of the random variable of interest. In this work, we explore the possibility of reconstruction, such that the reconstructed sample is still within a desired fidelity level with our original realization of the random variable, but at the same time, it resembles a sample from a different target distribution. We term this criterion as the deception constraint and find the fundamental tradeoffs of rate-distortion and deception.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [GAUGE: Grading Agent-Built Financial Models Without a Golden Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24889)
*Jiacheng Lu,Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Rui Sun,Cheng Hua,Tao Song,Hui Cai,Beidi Luan,Zhengze Wu,Lingjing Teng,Yijia He,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有金融模型评测基准通常以单一专家答案为标准，忽略了分析师之间普遍存在的合理分歧。本文提出GAUGE基准，基于1001份分析师工作簿和196项评估任务，采用三层观察实践包络、56个可审计维度、8个有效性门控和确定性结构检查来评估智能体构建的估值模型。结果显示，高级分析师平均得分88.3，初级分析师66.0，金融学生43.2，而最佳AI智能体得分53.4，虽高于学生均值但低于所有高级分析师和大多数初级分析师。当前智能体在模型构建方面远强于估值判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融评测基准通常以单一专家答案为评分标准，但金融模型中的预测、贴现率和目标价格往往存在多个合理答案，点容差评分会惩罚专业人士之间已存在的正常分歧，因此需要一种基于实际分析师实践的评测方法。

Method: GAUGE基准使用1001份供应商分类的分析师工作簿和196项评估任务，采用三层观察实践包络（而非单一点答案）、56个可审计方面、8个有效性门控和确定性结构检查。通过55名参与者的已知组别研究、公司分组交叉验证和评审稳定性审计进行验证。

Result: 在故障感知得分φ₀上，高级分析师平均88.3，初级分析师66.0，金融学生43.2。24个智能体和1011次生成中，最佳智能体得分53.4，高于学生均值但低于所有高级分析师和多数初级分析师。它通过93%的机械性方面和78%的判断性方面，整体中位差距为26分。现有智能体在模型构建方面远强于估值判断。

Conclusion: 当前AI智能体在金融建模的模型构建能力上表现较好，但在需要专业判断的估值环节仍显著落后于人类分析师。GAUGE基准提供了一种更符合实际行业实践的评估方法，并公开发布方法论、脱敏数据、训练集、评估核心和刷新池。

Abstract: Financial models combine public disclosures with analyst assumptions to produce forecasts and valuations. While some components can be checked mechanically, forecasts, discount rates, and target prices often admit multiple reasonable answers. Existing benchmarks nevertheless tend to grade such outputs against a single expert reference. Using independently built analyst models for the same companies, we find that across 108 directed pairs covering 65 companies, the median single-reference score is 0.33, 92.6% score below 0.70, and no same-vintage pair agrees on implied price within 10%. Point-tolerance grading can therefore penalize disagreement already present among professionals. We introduce GAUGE, a benchmark for evaluating agent-built valuation models against observed analyst practice rather than a single point answer. GAUGE uses 1,001 vendor-classified analyst workbooks and a 196-task evaluation set, with a three-layer observed-practice envelope, 56 auditable facets, eight validity gates, and deterministic structural checks. We validate the benchmark with a 55-participant known-groups study, company-grouped cross-fitting, and judge-stability audits. On the failure-aware score $φ_0$, senior analysts average 88.3, juniors 66.0, and finance students 43.2. Across 24 agents and 1,011 scored generations, the best agent scores 53.4, above the student mean but below every senior and most juniors. It passes 93% of mechanical facets and 78% of judgment facets, with a fleet-median gap of 26 points. Current agents are substantially stronger at model construction than valuation judgment. We release the methodology, a gated de-identified data tier, a controlled training split, a versioned 48-task evaluation core, and a withheld refresh pool.

</details>


### [262] [Score-Based Stabilization for Time-Dependent Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25119)
*Eshed Gal,Eldad Haber,Uri Ascher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分数模型（score model）的稳定化框架，用于偏微分方程（PDE）的数值模拟。该框架通过学习得到的分数模型定义稳定化算子，对数值更新进行修正，将其驱向允许状态流形，从而增强标准时间推进格式的鲁棒性和物理一致性。在Advection、KdV、NLS和Burgers方程上的实验表明，该方法能有效抑制非物理不稳定性并保留定性动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值格式在求解偏微分方程时容易出现非物理不稳定性，尤其是对非线性强或守恒性质敏感的方程。现有稳定化方法往往需要人工设计或问题特异的正则化项，缺乏通用性和自适应性。本文旨在利用数据驱动的分数模型，构建一种通用、自动的稳定化机制，使数值解保持在物理允许的流形上。

Method: 将分数模型（score model）作为稳定化算子，嵌入标准时间步进格式（如Runge-Kutta等）之后。具体而言，每次数值更新后，利用预训练的分数模型计算修正项，将数值解向高概率密度区域（即物理允许状态流形）投影。该修正操作被证明是一个收缩映射，具有盆地条件稳定性（basin-conditional stability）。

Result: 在Advection、KdV、NLS和Burgers方程上进行了数值实验。结果表明：所提框架有效抑制了非物理振荡和数值发散；在保持解的结构特征（如孤子、激波等）方面优于未稳定化的格式；对初始条件和时间步长具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于分数模型的稳定化框架为PDE数值模拟提供了一种数据驱动、问题无关的正则化策略。该方法的收缩性质保证了修正机制的理论稳定性，未来可推广至更复杂的PDE系统（如Navier-Stokes方程）以及多物理场耦合问题。

Abstract: We propose a score-based stabilization framework for numerical simulation of partial differential equations, in which a learned score model defines a stabilization operator applied to provisional numerical updates. This operator augments standard time-stepping schemes by enforcing structure and physical consistency through a correction that drives iterates toward the manifold of admissible states. We show that the stabilization operator acts as a contraction toward this manifold, yielding a correction mechanism with basin-conditional stability. Numerical experiments on Advection, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV), Nonlinear Schrodinger (NLS), and Burgers' equations demonstrate improved robustness, suppression of nonphysical instabilities, and preservation of qualitative dynamics.

</details>


### [263] [FinAbstain: Uncertainty-Calibrated Multimodal RAG for Selective Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24875)
*Dorothy Torres,Wei Cheng,Henan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FinAbstain是一个面向金融时间序列的不确定性校准多模态RAG框架，通过选择性预测机制（在不确定性高时弃权或转人工）来降低错误率，并采用模拟结果验证假设：校准弃权能以覆盖率为代价换取更低的选择性误差和回撤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLMs)在生成金融叙事时，当证据稀疏、过时或矛盾时往往会表现出过度自信，这在金融预测中尤其严重，因为财报、新闻、价格、成交量和技术信号之间常常不一致。现有方法缺乏对预测不确定性的系统校准和选择性预测机制。

Method: 提出了FinAbstain框架，包含：(1) 时间点检索器，仅检索预测时间戳之前公开的信息；(2) 多模态智能体（基本面、新闻、技术、风险、验证），提供模态特定的概率评估；(3) 通过检索相关性、证据矛盾、重复采样一致性和历史校准统计量聚合评估结果；(4) 评估多种校准方法（温度缩放、等渗回归、共形预测、混合不确定性分数）；(5) 控制器在不确定性低于阈值时预测（看涨/看跌/中性），否则弃权、请求证据、降低暴露或转人工审查。

Result: 在1天和5天异常收益方向、20天波动率区间、弃权决策上评估了准确率、校准度、风险-覆盖率、引用、交易、延迟和成本指标。使用明确标注的模拟结果（而非实证声明）验证了假设：校准的弃权策略能以覆盖率为代价换取更低的选择性误差和回撤。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于：(1) 时间安全的架构（避免未来信息泄露）；(2) 复合不确定性公式；(3) 可复现的评估蓝图，为证据驱动的选择性金融预测提供基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can synthesize financial narratives but may express high confidence when evidence is sparse, stale, or contradictory. This failure is especially consequential in forecasting, where filings, news, prices, volume, and technical signals can disagree. We present FinAbstain, a research framework for uncertainty-calibrated multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with selective prediction. A point-in-time retriever admits only information public at the forecast timestamp and supplies modality-specific evidence to fundamental, news, technical, risk, and verification agents. Their probabilistic assessments are aggregated with retrieval relevance, evidence contradiction, repeated-sample consistency, and historical calibration statistics. Temperature scaling, isotonic regression, conformal prediction, and a proposed hybrid uncertainty score are evaluated under a common chronological protocol. A controller predicts bullish, bearish, or neutral outcomes only when uncertainty is below a validated threshold; otherwise it abstains, requests evidence, reduces exposure, or routes the case to human review. The evaluation covers one- and five-day abnormal-return direction, twenty-day volatility intervals, and abstention decisions, using accuracy, calibration, risk--coverage, citation, trading, latency, and cost metrics. To make the design auditable before a full data collection is complete, we report explicitly labeled simulated results rather than empirical claims. These results illustrate the intended hypothesis: calibrated abstention may trade coverage for lower selective error and drawdown. The contribution is a time-safe architecture, a composite uncertainty formulation, and a reproducible evaluation blueprint for evidence-grounded selective financial forecasting.

</details>


### [264] [A Physics-Informed Neural Operator for Thermal Ranking of Low-Cost Wall Materials in Hot-Dry Climates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25668)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan,Fahim Raees,Ubaida Fatima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段计算框架，用于评估五种低成本本土墙材（泥砖、粘土-秸秆土坯、石灰稳定竹板、烧制粘土砖、石灰-泥复合材料）的热性能。第一阶段用Crank-Nicolson有限差分法求解一维瞬态热方程；第二阶段用物理信息神经算子（PINO）学习参数到解的映射。在极端高温干旱气候下，粘土-秸秆土坯具有最佳成本-性能比，而气候扫描揭示了热排除与热排斥两种工况下的材料排序反转规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在热带干旱气候区（夏季温度常超45°C），低收入农村住房需要识别经济可行的本土建筑材料以减少墙体热穿透，从而保障室内热舒适。当前缺乏能够系统评估低成本本土材料热性能并支持证据化选材的计算框架。

Method: 两阶段计算框架：1) 用Crank-Nicolson有限差分法求解带Robin边界条件的一维瞬态热方程，通过Latin Hypercube采样生成1500个周期日解；2) 基于Fourier Neural Operator (FNO)骨干的物理信息神经算子(PINO)，同时强制数据保真度和PDE一致性，学习参数到解的算子映射。

Result: PINO的相对L2场误差为5.14e-4，峰值内表面温度平均绝对误差0.201 K，完全保留FDM的材料排序；用150个FDM样本训练的PINO匹配用双倍数据训练的纯数据FNO；ISO 13786时间滞后重现精度达0.99 h，减幅因子达0.010；粘土-秸秆土坯在名义热干旱条件下成本-性能最优；气候扫描发现室外温度低于室内时材料排序反转，烧制粘土砖成为最优。

Conclusion: 该框架支持在数据稀缺情况下进行可靠的材料热性能评估和排序，特别是为后洪灾重建中的热干旱区低成本住房提供证据化材料选择依据。

Abstract: Identifying cost-effective indigenous building materials that minimise heat penetration through walls is critical for indoor thermal comfort in low-income rural housing in hot-dry climates, where summer temperatures routinely exceed 45 C. We present a two-stage computational framework for thermal ranking of five low-cost indigenous wall materials: mud brick, clay-straw adobe, lime-stabilised bamboo panel, fired clay brick, and lime-mud composite. First, a validated Crank-Nicolson finite difference method (FDM) solves the one-dimensional transient heat equation with Robin boundary conditions under diurnal solar and outdoor air-temperature forcing, generating 1500 periodic-day solutions across a nine-dimensional parameter space by Latin Hypercube sampling. Second, a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) with a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) backbone learns the parameter-to-solution operator mu -> T(x,t), enforcing both data fidelity and PDE consistency. The trained PINO attains a relative L2 field error of 5.14e-4 and a 0.201 K mean absolute error on the peak inner surface temperature, preserving the FDM material ranking exactly; PINO trained on 150 FDM samples matches a data-only FNO trained on twice as many, so the physics loss is most valuable when data are scarce. The periodic-day formulation also yields the ISO 13786 time lag and decrement factor, reproduced to within 0.99 h and 0.010. At nominal hot-dry summer conditions, clay-straw adobe achieves the best cost-performance index among widely available materials. A climate sweep, confirmed by FDM spot checks, reveals a regime boundary: under sub-ambient outdoor conditions the ranking inverts to conductive fired clay brick, delineating heat-exclusion and heat-rejection regimes. The framework supports evidence-based material selection for post-flood reconstruction in hot-dry regions.

</details>


### [265] [Human Preference aligned Tabular Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24880)
*Frederik Hoppe,Astrid Franz,Marianne Michaelis,Lars Kleinemeier,Udo Göbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有表格嵌入方法主要针对预测任务优化，而非生成符合人类偏好的相似性排序，并提出了一个包含人类偏好对齐评估的具体流程，以产品生命周期管理（PLM）用例说明该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务无关的表格嵌入方法虽广泛应用于产品生命周期管理（PLM）等实际业务系统的相似性搜索，但主要针对预测任务优化，未考虑生成符合人类偏好的相似性排序。作者认为标准下游指标不足以全面评估嵌入在相似性搜索中的可信度，需要引入人类偏好对齐的评估方法。

Method: 提出了一个具体的人类偏好对齐评估流程，通过PLM用例来展示现有嵌入方法在相似性排序中的不足。

Result: 标准下游指标不足以评估嵌入在相似性搜索中的可信度；当前嵌入方法缺乏对人类偏好的对齐。

Conclusion: 需要引入人类偏好对齐的评估方法作为表格嵌入在相似性搜索中可信度评估的必要组成部分。

Abstract: Task-agnostic tabular embeddings are increasingly used for similarity search in real-world business systems such as Product Lifecycle Management (PLM). However, leading embedding approaches are optimized primarily for prediction tasks - not for producing human preference aligned similarity rankings. We argue that standard downstream metrics are insufficient to fully assess embedding trustworthiness for similarity search and that human preference aligned evaluation is a necessary and currently missing component. We present a concrete evaluation procedure and illustrate the problem through a PLM use case.

</details>


### [266] [Behavior-Driven Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24881)
*Caroline Dominik,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于行为驱动开发（BDD）的结构化场景来生成系统行为解释的方法——行为驱动可解释性（BDX），并在RISC-V处理器异常解释的案例中验证了该方法在设计阶段的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统复杂度急剧增加，单个或单个团队难以全面理解系统的各个开发阶段（如设计、维护），尤其在安全关键系统中，最终设计必须可信，因此可解释性成为现代系统的重要需求。

Method: 利用行为驱动开发（BDD），以结构化场景的形式给出预期系统行为，每个功能对应一系列动作序列，并直接将场景翻译为解释，提出行为驱动可解释性（BDX）方法。

Result: 通过RISC-V处理器中异常解释的案例研究，验证了BDX方法在系统设计阶段提供支持的有效性。

Conclusion: BDX方法适用于任何开发阶段和抽象层级，能够提升系统可解释性，尤其有助于安全关键系统的信任建立。

Abstract: As system complexity has vastly increased, it has become significantly more challenging for a single person or a team to fully understand all aspects of an entire system. Particularly, this holds when considering all the different stages of a system's development life cycle, such as, e.g., design or maintenance. But especially for safety-critical systems it is essential that the final design can be trusted. Because of this, explainability is becoming an important requirement for modern systems. In this paper, we aim to achieve this goal by utilizing Behavior-Driven Development (BDD), where the expected system behavior is given in the form of structured scenarios. These scenarios give a sequence of actions for each functionality, and by this can be directly translated into explanations. We introduce this method of deriving explanations based on the specification as Behavior-Driven Explainability (BDX). While applicable at any development stage or abstraction level, a case study for the explanation of exceptions in a RISC-V processor shows the support this concept adds during system design.

</details>


### [267] [Eliminating Propagation Delay: Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24885)
*Jinpeng Chen,Ziyu Yu,Tao Wang,Jun Ma,Hongbo Gao,Senzhang Wang,Zufeng Zhang,Kaimin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为A-STFGCN的注意力机制时空融合图卷积网络，通过设计时空融合模块消除传播延迟误差，并利用基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制捕获长短时时间特征，在五个真实数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图卷积模型主要关注拓扑结构中的静态空间依赖和时空相关性，忽视了节点间信息传播的延迟差异，且大量复杂结构堆叠导致计算时间增加，不能满足模型的时效性需求。

Method: 提出Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network (A-STFGCN)，设计时空融合模块提取去除传播延迟误差的时空特征相关性，并基于掩码矩阵的多头自注意力机制同时捕获长短时时间特征。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，与八种基线方法相比，该方法在整体性能最优的同时，具有良好的计算效率和数据利用效率。

Conclusion: A-STFGCN通过解决传播延迟误差和模型时效性问题，有效提升了交通流预测的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Predicting traffic flow is crucial to optimizing transportation systems and improving urban mobility. Many graph convolution-based models have been proposed to extract spatial-temporal features and predict traffic flow. However, most focus on spatial-temporal and semantic correlation in topological relationships. There are two primary problems to address. Firstly, the convolutional structure in the model focuses on utilizing static spatial dependencies and spatial-temporal relationships in topological structures, while neglecting the different information propagation delays between adjacent nodes in the convolution. Secondly, these methods often stack a large number of complex structures, resulting in a substantial increase in computational time during the model training phase, thereby disregarding the model's requirements for timeliness. In this paper, we propose a novel network called the Attention-Based Spatial-Temporal Fusion Graph Convolution Network (A-STFGCN). We design a spatial-temporal fusion block to extract the spatial-temporal feature correlations with propagation delay errors removed and to capture both long-term and short-term temporal characteristics of the data within a multi-head self-attention mechanism based on a mask matrix. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves the best overall performance while having good computation and data utilization efficiency compared with the eight baseline methods.

</details>


### [268] [Mechanisms of Width Scaling in Normalized Residual Networks: The Effective Alignment Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24887)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Guangming Tan,Fangming Liu,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了残差扩展中训练与测试梯度对齐的理论问题，提出了有效对齐维度这一可测量量，并基于有限样本上界推导了测试风险改进的高概率条件。实验验证了该理论在多种网络架构中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络宽度理论仅刻画渐近极限，无法解释有限训练数据下识别出的扩展方向是否能在未见数据上保持有益效果。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 推导了独立估计的训练与测试梯度之间内积的精确均值和方差，得到误对齐概率的有限样本上界；该上界仅依赖于有效对齐维度和有效样本量，无需协方差谱假设或预设宽度增长速率。将该证书集成到训练-测试残差扩展框架中。

Result: 更宽的模型表现出更大的有效对齐维度和更低的经验误对齐。直接残差干预实验证实，对齐统计量能够预测留出集损失变化的符号和幅度。

Conclusion: 有效对齐维度是描述激活梯度信号-噪声几何结构的可测量量，为有限数据下的残差扩展方向可靠性提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Existing theories of neural-network width characterize asymptotic limits, but provide limited guidance on whether an expansion direction identified from finite training data remains beneficial on unseen data. We study this problem for function-preserving residual expansion and introduce the effective alignment dimension, a measurable quantity describing the signal-noise geometry of activation gradients. By deriving the exact mean and variance of the inner product between independently estimated training and test gradients, we obtain a finite-sample upper bound on misalignment probability. The bound depends only on the effective alignment dimension and an effective sample size, requiring finite second moments and a nonzero population gradient, without covariance spectral assumptions or prescribed width-growth rates. We integrate this certificate into the train-test residual-expansion framework, yielding a high-probability condition for test-risk improvement. Experiments across width-controlled LLaMA-style Transformers, Pythia, and ResNet-20 show that wider models exhibit larger effective alignment dimensions and lower empirical misalignment. Direct residual interventions confirm that the alignment statistic predicts the sign and magnitude of held-out loss changes.

</details>


### [269] [LLM as Forecasting Planner: Training-Free Text Conditioning for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24892)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAFP方法，将文本条件时间序列预测建模为规划问题，使用冻结的TSFM作为模拟器生成数值轨迹，LLM作为策略和价值函数通过MCTS引导候选选择，在不重新训练模型的情况下弥合模态差距，实验表明该方法在不同模型组合下均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFM）擅长数值预测，大语言模型（LLM）能理解文本上下文，但直接让LLM生成或修改预测值会破坏TSFM捕捉的时间结构。现有方法难以有效结合二者的优势。

Method: 将预测建模为规划问题：冻结的TSFM作为模拟器生成数值延续轨迹；LLM作为策略函数（Ranker）和值函数（Judge）引导候选选择；通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）在预测时域上进行搜索，无需训练或微调任一模型。

Result: 在Context-is-Key和Time-MMD两个基准上，使用Chronos和TimesFM两种TSFM骨干及四种LLM，LAFP在所有模型组合下均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 将文本条件预测转化为规划问题，通过MCTS结合TSFM和LLM是一种有效的免训练方法，序列搜索可以在不重新训练的情况下弥合模态差距。

Abstract: Text-conditioned time-series forecasting predicts a series from both its numerical history and natural-language context, allowing forecasts to account for events and constraints that the past alone cannot reveal. This requires both reliable numerical forecasting and the ability to interpret contextual information. Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide strong numerical forecasts, while large language models (LLMs) can reason over text, but combining their strengths remains challenging because asking an LLM to generate or revise forecast values directly can distort the temporal structure captured by the TSFM. We instead formulate forecasting as a planning problem over TSFM-generated trajectories. The frozen TSFM acts as a simulator that proposes numerical continuations, while the LLM acts as a policy and value function that guides candidate selection and evaluates completed trajectories against the context. We instantiate this as \rc{} (\textbf{L}LM \textbf{A}s \textbf{F}orecasting \textbf{P}lanner), a training-free framework that bridges the modality gap without retraining either model, using Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) over the forecast horizon with a \emph{Ranker} LLM as policy and a \emph{Judge} LLM as value function. Experiments on Context-is-Key and Time-MMD across two TSFM backbones (Chronos and TimesFM) and four LLMs show that \rc{} delivers consistent improvements across model choices, supporting sequential search as an effective training-free approach to text-conditioned forecasting.

</details>


### [270] [Inverse RL Helps Align AI by Imitating Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24900)
*Michał Wiliński,Liu Leqi,Chirag Nagpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PARED通过逆强化学习从演示中提取隐式奖励函数，无需偏好标注，可应用于推理时重排和对抗性在线强化学习，提升基础策略性能并支持上下文对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐方法依赖监督微调或基于人类反馈的强化学习，但演示数据本身是否能产生可检查、可复用、可在线优化的隐式奖励尚不清楚。

Method: 提出PARED方法，利用轻量级判别器在响应级特征空间中区分演示与策略自采样，恢复演示背后的隐式奖励作为显式函数；无需任务特定偏好标注，可结合AI反馈扩展监督维度。

Result: 在推理时重排和对抗性在线RL实验中，PARED恢复的奖励无需监督损失即可提升基础策略，且在标准SFT后进一步优化可带来额外增益；同时支持上下文对齐，使单一策略适应不同受众偏好。

Conclusion: 演示数据本身蕴含可提取的隐式奖励，PARED有效利用这一资源实现了灵活、高效的对齐方法。

Abstract: Language model alignment aims to make model behavior reliably reflect desirable properties such as helpfulness, safety, and instruction following. Current approaches typically use supervised fine-tuning on demonstrations or reinforcement learning with rewards derived from verifiers or human feedback. These paradigms leave an important question underexplored: can demonstrations alone yield an implicit reward that can be inspected, reused, and optimized on-policy to align AI? Motivated by inverse reinforcement learning, we introduce Projected Alignment Reward Estimated from Demonstrations (PARED). PARED recovers the implicit reward underlying expert demonstrations as an explicit function over a small set of response-level features, learned by a lightweight discriminator that separates demonstrations from the policy's own samples in this feature space. Unlike a standard reward model, PARED requires no task-specific preference annotations: demonstrations provide the task-specific supervision, which can be augmented with AI feedback as additional dimensions of supervision. Through experiments involving inference-time reranking and adversarial on-policy RL, we show that the recovered reward improves a base policy without a supervised loss and yields further gains when optimized after standard supervised fine-tuning. Additionally, we demonstrate that PARED can be used for contextual alignment, in which a single policy can be tailored to the preferences of different audiences.

</details>


### [271] [Multiclass Classification without Labels via Posterior Simplex Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24943)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Troels Petersen,Vincenzo Piuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将二元分类中的CWoLa原理扩展到多分类场景（K>2），证明在仅知混合身份而不知真实标签和类别先验的情况下，通过训练区分不同未标记混合样本的分类器，可在(K-1)单纯形中恢复潜在类别结构。实验表明该方法能有效缩小弱监督与全监督学习的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多分类问题缺乏可靠的实例级标签，但可通过不同筛选条件、来源或实验条件构建弱增强的未标记样本。现有CWoLa仅适用于二元分类，本文旨在将其推广到多分类场景，使学习者能从多个未标记混合物中恢复潜在类别结构。

Method: 证明多分类混合模型下贝叶斯最优混合分类器g*将数据点映射到嵌入混合后验空间的(K-1)单纯形中，其K个顶点由潜在类别通过未知混合矩阵诱导。提出无需先验的两类方法：后验单纯形拟合和瓶颈架构，通过训练标准分类器区分混合身份，再提取潜在类别结构。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和Galaxy10 DECaLS数据集上的实验表明，仅利用混合身份即可恢复潜在类别及其在混合物中的比例，有效缩小了弱监督与全监督学习之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 本文提供了数学上严谨且可扩展的多类别发现工具，适用于标签稀缺领域，将CWoLa原理成功推广到多类场景。

Abstract: In many classification problems, reliable instance-level labels are unavailable. However, it is often possible to construct weakly enriched unlabeled samples: datasets selected by different cuts, sources, populations, or experimental conditions that change latent class proportions without revealing them. Classification without Labels (CWoLa) shows that, in the binary case ($K=2$), a classifier trained to distinguish two impure mixtures with different class proportions can recover an optimal class discriminator without knowing the mixture proportions. We extend this principle to multiclass learning from several unlabeled mixtures ($K>2$), where the learner observes only mixture identity and neither latent class labels nor class-prior matrices. We prove that, for a multiclass mixture model, the Bayes-optimal mixture classifier $g^\star$ maps data points into a $(K-1)$-simplex embedded in mixture-posterior space. The $K$ vertices of this simplex are induced by the latent classes through the unknown mixing matrix. Leveraging this geometry, we propose prior-free procedures that train a standard classifier to distinguish mixture identities and then extract latent class structure using either post-hoc simplex fitting or a bottleneck architecture. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Galaxy10 DECaLS show that mixture identity alone can recover latent classes and their fractions in the mixture. By narrowing the gap between weakly supervised and fully supervised performance, we provide a mathematically grounded, scalable tool for multiclass discovery in label-scarce domains.

</details>


### [272] [Stable FP4 Training via Transposition-Invariant Block Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24953)
*Mehdi Rahimifar,Amin Darabi,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Xing Huang,Yao Wang,Zhijun Tu,Yufei Cui,Yunke Peng,Hongliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于2D块FP4量化的低精度训练框架，通过强制前向-后向缩放一致性解决FP4训练中的不稳定问题，在7B密集模型和30B MoE模型上实现稳定训练，性能与BF16相当（困惑度和准确率退化<1.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将训练精度从FP8降低到FP4面临优化不稳定的挑战，现有1D块量化方案中张量转置导致前向和后向传播对相同值分配不同缩放因子，产生有偏且不稳定的梯度更新。

Method: 提出2D块FP4量化框架：（1）2D块量化实现转置不变缩放，保持前向后向一致性；（2）结合无截断缩放和随机舍入控制量化误差并保持无偏梯度；（3）对注意力机制的Q和K投影采用MXFP8量化，形成混合精度设计。

Result: 在7B密集LLM和30B MoE模型上训练至100B token，FP4训练稳定且与BF16性能匹配，困惑度和下游准确率退化小于1.3%。

Conclusion: 强制前向-后向缩放一致性足以实现可扩展的实用FP4训练，为更高效的LLM训练提供了简单有效的路径。

Abstract: Reducing training precision is a key lever for improving the e ciency of large language model (LLM) training, but pushing beyond FP8 to 4-bit oating point (FP4) remains challenging due to instability during optimization. We identify a fundamental source of this instability in existing microscaling approaches: scale inconsistency induced by tensor transposition. In conventional 1D block quantization, forward and backward passes assign di erent scaling factors to the same values after transposition, leading to biased and unstable gradient updates. To address this issue, we propose a low-precision training framework based on 2D block FP4 quantization, which enforces transposition-invariant scaling and preserves consistency between forward and backward computations. We further combine this with truncation-free scaling and stochastic rounding to control quantization error and maintain unbiased gradients. To handle the sensitivity of attention mechanisms, we adopt MXFP8 quantization for query and key projections, yielding a practical mixed-precision design. We evaluate our method on dense LLMs up to 7B parameters and a 30B Mixture-of-Experts model, trained on up to 100B tokens. Across all settings, our approach achieves stable end-to-end FP4 training and closely matches BF16 performance, with less than 1.3% degradation in perplexity and downstream accuracy. These results demonstrate that enforcing forwardbackward scaling consistency is su cient to enable practical FP4 training at scale, providing a simple and e ective pathway toward more e cient LLM training.

</details>


### [273] [Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24983)
*Ziwei Zhang,Jonathan Yu-Meng Li,Zhihao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GDRO框架，通过采样器-Sinkhorn配对，在无需似然函数的情况下，将最坏情况分布约束在条件生成器族内，实现分布鲁棒优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRO方法在模型兼容性与对抗结构之间存在权衡：接受任意采样的方法不限制最坏情况分布，而生成器参数化的对抗方法依赖似然、分数或训练数据等模型特定访问。

Method: 提出GDRO框架，核心是采样器-Sinkhorn配对：采样器精确表示条件分布律，Sinkhorn散度无需似然即可比较分布并可从样本估计。采用可微分的原始-对偶实现。

Result: 对于Lipschitz损失，人口Sinkhorn半径约束下游性能退化。在显式和隐式生成器上，方法将稀有上下文库存遗憾降低60%，SocialGAN导航碰撞减少50%。

Conclusion: GDRO是一个兼容任意可采样条件生成器的原则性DRO框架，通过Sinkhorn散度实现无需似然访问的分布鲁棒优化，有效降低了罕见上下文下的决策遗憾。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly adopted in distributionally robust optimization (DRO), but existing approaches trade off model compatibility and adversarial structure: methods that accept arbitrary samplers do not restrict worst-case laws to a generator family, while generator-parameterized adversaries rely on model-specific access such as likelihoods, scores, or training data. We propose Generative Distributionally Robust Optimization (GDRO), a principled framework that accepts any sampleable conditional generator as the nominal model and restricts worst-case laws to a chosen conditional generator family. The key is the sampler-Sinkhorn pairing: samplers represent the conditional laws exactly, while Sinkhorn divergence compares their induced distributions without likelihood access and can be estimated from samples alone. The resulting population problem admits a direct finite-sample approximation and differentiable primal-dual implementation at the active decision context. For Lipschitz losses, the population Sinkhorn radius bounds downstream degradation. Across explicit and implicit generators, our method reduces rare-context inventory regret by 60% and SocialGAN navigation collisions by 50% relative to nominal decisions.

</details>


### [274] [Calibrated Partial Resets: Preventing Policy Collapse in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24996)
*Luc McCutcheon,Evangelos Chatzaroulas,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Calibrated Partial Resets (CPR)优化器，通过按神经元效用度缩放地向初始化方向拉动低效用神经元，在保持塑性的同时避免训练不稳定，在多种持续学习基准上优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在非平稳数据（持续学习、强化学习）中会出现休眠神经元累积和表达能力下降的问题。现有神经元重置方法虽能恢复塑性，但完全重置会牺牲峰值性能并导致策略崩溃。

Method: CPR优化器定期将低效用神经元向初始化方向拉动，拉动强度由每个神经元的效用度缩放。不同于二值重置（全或无）和均匀衰减，该方法仅集中调整最需要调整的单元。

Result: 在SlipperyAnt任务上，CPR是唯一能在4亿训练步中避免策略崩溃的方法；在Continual MetaWorld和Continual MinAtar基准上优于先前的衰减和重置方法。

Conclusion: 消融实验揭示了塑性与峰值性能之间的可调权衡，突出了效用度缩放的重新初始化作为持续学习中有前景的方向。

Abstract: Neural networks are hindered by accumulating dormant neurons and loss of expressivity throughout training, particularly in non-stationary data settings, such as continual supervised and reinforcement learning. Recently, neuron resets have been used to maintain gradient flow and restore plasticity. However, full unit reinitialization often sacrifices peak performance and can destabilize training, leading to policy collapse.
  To preserve plasticity without destabilizing training, we propose Calibrated Partial Resets (CPR), an optimizer that periodically pulls low-utility neurons toward their initialization, with pull strength scaled by each neuron's utility. Unlike binary reset methods, partial resets avoid brittleness; unlike uniform decay, calibrated utility-scaling concentrates adjustment on the units that need it most.
  Among compared methods, only CPR avoids policy collapse over 400M training steps in SlipperyAnt, and it outperforms prior decay and reset-based methods on Continual MetaWorld and Continual MinAtar benchmarks. Ablations reveal a tunable trade-off between plasticity and peak performance, highlighting utility-scaled reinitialization as a promising direction for continual learning.

</details>


### [275] [Conformal Cascade: Distribution-Free Accuracy Guarantees for Multi-Tier LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25018)
*Yifan Dou,Shikan Fang,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**Conformal Cascade (CC)**，一种基于共形预测的多层LLM级联推理框架。CC使用共形预测集的大小作为拒绝规则：当校准后的预测集坍缩为单个答案时接受小模型输出，否则退回大模型。该方法无需模型训练，仅需黑盒API访问，提供分布无关、有限样本的准确性保证，并在18个多选基准测试中优于基于阈值调优的启发式级联方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM级联方法存在三个问题：(1) 使用置信度阈值来拒绝查询，但LLM置信度分数校准不良；(2) 阈值需要针对每对模型和每个领域手动调整；(3) 没有任何设置能为级联的准确性提供形式化保证。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出一个具有统计保证、无需调优的级联框架。

Method: CC使用共形预测集大小作为延迟规则：若校准后的预测集坍缩为单一答案则接受当前层输出，否则转向更大模型。通过每层联合界（per-tier union bound）提供分布无关的有限样本准确性保证：接受层的预测集以至少$1-Kα$的概率覆盖正确答案；在特定条件下，该界可收紧至$1-α$。进一步刻画了预期级联成本作为$α$和校准集接受率的显式函数。

Result: 在18个多选基准（涵盖科学、医学、常识、标准化考试）上，使用4个开源模型系列构建的两层级联进行评估。CC在大多数模型-基准对（family-benchmark pairs）上严格优于最强的基于校准阈值调优的启发式级联方法。在推理密集型基准上提升最大（多数投票不可靠时）；在简单基准上，绝大多数查询由小模型处理且无准确性损失。

Conclusion: CC提供了一种无需训练、仅需黑盒API访问的LLM级联新范式，以统计保证替代经验调优，在推理成本和准确性之间取得了更好的平衡。扩展到开放式生成需要答案聚类步骤，留待未来工作。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) cascades reduce inference cost by routing easy queries to a small model and deferring hard queries to a larger one. Production cascades govern this deferral through a confidence threshold, but LLM confidence scores are miscalibrated, the threshold must be tuned per model pair and per domain, and no setting yields a formal bound on cascade accuracy. We introduce \textbf{Conformal Cascade} (CC), a multi-tier inference framework that uses conformal prediction set size as the deferral rule: accept when the calibrated set collapses to a single answer, defer otherwise. The procedure delivers a distribution-free, finite-sample accuracy guarantee. By a per-tier union bound, the prediction set at the accepting tier covers the correct answer with probability at least $1 - Kα$ for any user-specified $α$; under a selection-preservation condition (consistent with, but not strictly implied by, our marginal coverage results), the bound tightens to $1 - α$. We further characterise expected cascade cost as an explicit function of $α$ and the calibration-set acceptance rate. Across 18 multiple-choice benchmarks spanning science, medicine, commonsense, and standardized exams, evaluated on two-tier cascades drawn from four open-weight model families, CC strictly improves over the strongest calibration-tuned heuristic cascade on the majority of family--benchmark pairs, with the largest gains on reasoning-heavy benchmarks where majority vote is unreliable; on easier benchmarks the cascade commits the vast majority of queries to the small model at no accuracy cost. Extension to open-ended generation requires an answer-clustering step that we leave for future work. The method requires no model training and only black-box API access.

</details>


### [276] [Lantern: Conflict-Aware Gradient Blending for Physics-Guided Diffusion Models in Calorimeter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25060)
*Farzana Yasmin Ahmad,Vanamala Venkataswamy,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lantern，一种物理引导的扩散模型，用于高能物理量热器簇射的蒙特卡洛模拟。针对纯统计去噪目标无法保证物理正确性的问题，引入Correlation Frobenius Distance (CFD)指标和两类物理感知辅助损失（方差稳定的体素残差损失和基于探测器几何的图拉普拉斯损失），并通过GradBlend方法将物理信号注入去噪过程。实验表明，相比现有多任务梯度方法，Lantern在FPD和CFD指标上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型作为快速替代模型已用于量热器簇射模拟，但其去噪目标纯粹基于统计，模型可能在最小化损失的同时产生物理错误。现有物理信息生成方法假设存在封闭形式的物理定律、PDE残差或硬约束，而簇射过程没有逐样本PDE控制，能量守恒也只约束一个标量。标准评估指标忽略层间和体素间的相关结构，仅在物理特征空间比较簇射。

Method: 提出Correlation Frobenius Distance (CFD)作为层维和体素维相关性的归一化评分指标。设计两类物理感知辅助损失：基于计数统计的方差稳定化体素残差损失，和基于探测器几何的图拉普拉斯损失。通过GradBlend方法结合两者与去噪目标，锚定步长于去噪梯度同时让辅助损失引导方向。

Result: 在CaloChallenge Dataset 2上，PCGrad、GradNorm、IMTL-G、ConFIG等任务对称规则注入物理损失会使FPD比纯去噪增大2-100倍，而GradBlend在不退化的情况下引入物理信号。结合图拉普拉斯损失的Lantern同时提升了FPD和CFD。消融实验表明，与去噪梯度冲突的体素残差损失需要终端纯去噪阶段来保持簇射保真度，而无冲突的图拉普拉斯损失对调度不敏感。

Conclusion: GradBlend方法能够有效将冲突性物理先验注入扩散模型而不会导致性能退化，基于图拉普拉斯的几何损失是最稳定有效的物理引导信号。相关工作为高能物理模拟中物理信息生成模型提供了新的方法论和评估框架。
